# Great creatives direct. 
They don't prompt.

Rainfrog is built for art directors, not engineers. Drop your assets, mix and match, and generate visuals that look like they were art directed — because they were.

[Get Started](https://app.rainfrog.ai/signup) [Sign Up](https://app.rainfrog.ai/signup)

Watch our film ↓

## Workflows

NO PROMPT ENGINEERING

## Give directions. Rainfrog does the work.

You don't need to write prompts. Rainfrog builds them automatically in the backend—with taste already embedded. Connect your assets, point the workflow, and generate.

FAQ

## Frequently asked questions

Quick answers about Rainfrog, workflows, pricing, and how the platform fits creative teams.

What is Rainfrog?+

Rainfrog is a web-based creative platform for art directors and brand teams. You connect products, characters, styles, and environments in a visual node workflow, then generate campaign-ready images and video without prompt engineering.

Who is Rainfrog built for?+

Rainfrog is designed for art directors, creative directors, and marketing teams who need art-directed AI output at scale. It is built for creative direction workflows—not for developers writing custom AI pipelines.

How do Rainfrog workflows work?+

You build a node graph on the canvas by connecting asset nodes (product, model, style, environment) to generator nodes. Rainfrog traverses the graph, assembles prompts automatically, runs image or video generation, and saves results to your project library.

Is Rainfrog free to try?+

Yes. You can create a free account at app.rainfrog.ai to explore the studio. Paid subscription plans (Hobby, Pro, and Ultra) add monthly credits, storage, and production features for ongoing work.

What subscription plans does Rainfrog offer?+

Rainfrog offers Hobby, Pro, and Ultra monthly or annual plans with increasing credits and storage. Visit the pricing page for current rates, credit allotments, and feature comparison across tiers.

What AI models does Rainfrog use?+

Rainfrog combines model providers for different tasks. Depending on the specific workflow and desired output, Rainfrog picks the best model. You can always choose normal or pro quality per generation node.
